        t.say(fmt.Sprintf("Давай! Пункт чек-листа «Расскажи о %s» — я послушаю. %s\n\nЕсли захочешь выйти — напиши «отмена».", rub.Title, rub.Prompt))
        t.suggest("Отмена")
        a.notif.Track(ctx, t.u, "teachback_started", map[string]any{"topic": topic})
}

func (a *Agent) gradeTeachback(ctx context.Context, t *turn) {
        topic := t.state["topic"]
        rub, ok := a.cat.Rubrics[topic]
        if !ok {
                t.newSt = map[string]string{}
                a.handleQuestion(ctx, t)
                return
        }
        t.p.Intent = "teachback.grade"
        explanation := strings.TrimSpace(t.state["acc"] + "\n" + t.text)
        if len(strings.Fields(t.text)) < 6 && t.state["acc"] == "" {
                t.newSt = t.state
                t.say("Расскажи чуть подробнее — хотя бы 3–4 предложения своими словами. Не страшно ошибиться: я подскажу, что упущено.")
                t.suggest("Отмена")
                return
        }
        covered := a.grade(ctx, t, rub, explanation)
        res := domain.TeachbackResult{Topic: topic, Title: rub.Title, DocID: rub.Doc}
        for _, c := range rub.Concepts {
                cr := domain.ConceptResult{ID: c.ID, Label: c.Label}
                if covered[c.ID] {
                        res.Covered = append(res.Covered, cr)
                } else {
                        cr.Hint = c.Hint
                        res.Missing = append(res.Missing, cr)
                }
        }
        res.Score = len(res.Covered) * 100 / len(rub.Concepts)
        res.Passed = float64(len(res.Covered)) >= rub.PassRatio*float64(len(rub.Concepts))
        t.card(domain.Card{Type: "teachback", Teachback: &res})
        a.cite(t, rub.Doc)
        a.notif.Track(ctx, t.u, "teachback_graded", map[string]any{"topic": topic, "score": res.Score, "passed": res.Passed, "mode": t.p.Mode})
        if !res.Passed {
                t.newSt = map[string]string{"await": "teachback", "topic": topic, "task": t.state["task"], "acc": textutil.Truncate(explanation, 1500)}
                t.say(fmt.Sprintf("Хорошее начало: раскрыто %d из %d пунктов (%d%%). Чтобы засчитать, нужно хотя бы %d. Подсказки — в карточке. Допиши, что упустил — я учту всё вместе.",
                        len(res.Covered), len(rub.Concepts), res.Score, int(rub.PassRatio*float64(len(rub.Concepts))+0.999)))
                t.suggest("Отмена")
                return
        }
        t.newSt = map[string]string{}
        t.say(fmt.Sprintf("Отлично! Ты раскрыл %d из %d пунктов (%d%%) — этого достаточно, чтобы засчитать «Расскажи о %s».", len(res.Covered), len(rub.Concepts), res.Score, rub.Title))
        taskID := t.state["task"]
        if taskID == "" {
                if task, ok := a.teachTask(ctx, t, topic); ok {
                        taskID = task.ID
                }
        }
        if taskID == "" {
                t.say("Этот пункт уже отмечен в чек-листе — можно просто гордиться собой.")
                return
        }
        act, err := a.actions.PlanChecklist(ctx, t.u, t.u, taskID, fmt.Sprintf("Teach-back в Тропе: %d%% рубрики", res.Score))
        if err != nil {
                t.say("Не получилось подготовить отметку в чек-листе — попробуй позже.")
                return
        }
        t.say("Засчитать пункт в чек-листе новичка?")
        t.action(act)
}

// grade returns covered concept ids: LLM when available, keyword rubric otherwise.
func (a *Agent) grade(ctx context.Context, t *turn, rub *catalog.Rubric, text string) map[string]bool {
        covered := map[string]bool{}
        norm := textutil.Normalize(text)
        for _, c := range rub.Concepts {
                if textutil.ContainsAny(norm, c.Keywords) {
                        covered[c.ID] = true
                }
        }
        if !a.llmOn(ctx, t.u) {
                return covered
        }
        var concepts []llm.Concept
        for _, c := range rub.Concepts {
                concepts = append(concepts, llm.Concept{ID: c.ID, Label: c.Label})
        }
        g, err := a.llm.Grade(ctx, llm.GradeRequest{Topic: rub.Title, Explanation: text, Concepts: concepts})
        if err != nil {
                a.log.Warn("llm grade failed, keyword rubric", "err", err)
                t.p.Mode = ModeLLMFallback
                return covered
        }
        t.p.Mode = ModeLLM
        llmCovered := map[string]bool{}
        for _, id := range g.Covered {
                llmCovered[id] = true
        }
        return llmCovered
}

// Package app wires all components together.
package app

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "log/slog"
        "net/http"
        "os"
        "time"

        "github.com/prometheus/client_golang/prometheus"
        "github.com/prometheus/client_golang/prometheus/promhttp"
        "github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"

        "tropa/content"
        "tropa/internal/actions"
        "tropa/internal/agent"
        "tropa/internal/auth"
        "tropa/internal/catalog"
        "tropa/internal/clock"
        "tropa/internal/corp"
        "tropa/internal/corpmock"
        "tropa/internal/cron"
        "tropa/internal/directory"
        "tropa/internal/flags"
        "tropa/internal/httpapi"
        "tropa/internal/insights"
        "tropa/internal/kb"
        "tropa/internal/llm"
        "tropa/internal/mcp"
        "tropa/internal/mentor"
        "tropa/internal/notify"
        "tropa/internal/policy"
        "tropa/internal/ratelimit"
        "tropa/internal/route"
        "tropa/internal/seed"
        "tropa/internal/store"
        "tropa/internal/webui"
)

// App is the assembled application.
type App struct {
        Cfg      Config
        Log      *slog.Logger
        Store    *store.Store
        Clock    clock.Clock
        Sim      *clock.Sim
        Dir      *directory.Directory
        Catalog  *catalog.Catalog
        KB       *kb.Index
        MCP      *mcp.Client
        CorpMock *corpmock.Mock // non-nil in embedded mode
        Admin    corp.Admin
        Actions  *actions.Service
        Mentor   *mentor.Service
        Cron     *cron.Cron
        Handler  http.Handler
        redis    *redis.Client
}

// New builds the app; st may be injected (tests) or nil to open cfg.DatabaseURL.
func New(ctx context.Context, cfg Config, st *store.Store, log *slog.Logger) (*App, error) {
        a := &App{Cfg: cfg, Log: log}
        var err error
        if st == nil {
                if st, err = store.Open(ctx, cfg.DatabaseURL, cfg.DBSchema); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                if err := st.Migrate(ctx); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
        }
        a.Store = st
        if a.Catalog, err = catalog.Load(content.FS); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        a.Dir = directory.New()
        pol := policy.New(a.Dir)

        // clock: simulated in demo mode (time machine), wall clock otherwise
        if cfg.DemoMode {
                a.Sim = clock.NewSim(cfg.SimStart, nil)
                if base, setAt, ok, err := st.LoadClock(ctx); err == nil && ok {
                        a.Sim.Set(base.Add(time.Since(setAt)))
                } else {
                        _ = st.SaveClock(ctx, cfg.SimStart, time.Now())
                }
                a.Clock = a.Sim
        } else {
                a.Clock = clock.Real{}
        }

        // corporate services over MCP
        if cfg.CorpMCPURL == "" {
                a.CorpMock = corpmock.New(a.Dir, a.Catalog, cfg.CorpToken, a.Clock.Now)
                a.CorpMock.Latency = cfg.CorpLatency
                a.MCP = mcp.NewClient("http://corp.internal/mcp", cfg.CorpToken, 15*time.Second)
                a.MCP.HTTP.Transport = mcp.HandlerTransport{Handler: a.CorpMock.Handler(cfg.CorpToken)}
                a.Admin = mockAdmin{a.CorpMock}
        } else {
                a.MCP = mcp.NewClient(cfg.CorpMCPURL, cfg.CorpToken, 15*time.Second)
                a.Admin = corp.NewHTTPAdmin(cfg.CorpMCPURL, cfg.CorpToken)
        }
        corpSvc := &corp.Client{MCP: a.MCP}

        // rate limiting / cron leader lock
        var limiter ratelimit.Limiter
        var locker ratelimit.Locker
        if cfg.RedisURL != "" {
                opt, err := redis.ParseURL(cfg.RedisURL)
                if err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                a.redis = redis.NewClient(opt)
                r := &ratelimit.Redis{C: a.redis}
                limiter, locker = r, r
        } else {
                m := ratelimit.NewMemory()
                limiter, locker = m, m
        }

        var provider llm.Provider
        llmName := "offline"
        if cfg.LLMProvider == "anthropic" {
                p := llm.NewAnthropic(llm.AnthropicConfig{APIKey: cfg.AnthropicKey, BaseURL: cfg.AnthropicURL, Model: cfg.LLMModel, Effort: cfg.LLMEffort, ServerFallback: cfg.LLMFallback})
                provider, llmName = p, p.Name()
        }

        notifier := notify.New(st, a.Clock, log)
        fl := flags.New(st)
        a.KB = kb.New(a.Catalog, a.Dir, pol)
        rt := route.New(a.Catalog, a.Dir, pol, st)
        a.Mentor = mentor.New(st, a.Clock, a.Dir, pol, notifier, a.KB)
        a.Actions = actions.New(st, a.Clock, a.Dir, a.Catalog, pol, corpSvc, notifier, fl, a.Mentor, rt, log)
        ins := insights.New(st, a.Clock, a.Dir, a.Catalog, pol, rt, a.KB)
        ag := agent.New(agent.Deps{Store: st, Clock: a.Clock, Dir: a.Dir, Catalog: a.Catalog, Policy: pol, KB: a.KB, Route: rt, LLM: provider,
                Flags: fl, Actions: a.Actions, Notify: notifier, Log: log})
        a.Cron = cron.New(st, a.Clock, a.Dir, rt, a.Actions, notifier, locker, log)

        if err := seed.Run(ctx, st); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if err := a.Mentor.RebuildIndex(ctx); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }

        reg := prometheus.NewRegistry()
        reg.MustRegister(prometheus.NewGoCollector())
        metrics := httpapi.NewMetrics(reg)
        api := httpapi.New(httpapi.Deps{Auth: auth.New(cfg.SessionSecret, a.Dir, 8*time.Hour, cfg.SecureCookie), Dir: a.Dir, Catalog: a.Catalog, Policy: pol,
                Store: st, Clock: a.Clock, Route: rt, Agent: ag, Actions: a.Actions, Mentor: a.Mentor, Insights: ins, Flags: fl, Notify: notifier,
                Limiter: limiter, Metrics: metrics, Demo: &demo{a: a}, LLMName: llmName, Log: log, Ready: a.ready})
        mux := http.NewServeMux()
        api.Register(mux)
        mux.Handle("GET /metrics", promhttp.HandlerFor(reg, promhttp.HandlerOpts{}))
        mux.HandleFunc("/api/", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
                w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
                w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNotFound)
                _, _ = w.Write([]byte(`{"error":{"code":"not_found","message":"Неизвестный метод API"}}`))
        })
        mux.Handle("/", webui.Handler())
        a.Handler = httpapi.Middleware(mux, metrics, log)
        return a, nil
}

func (a *App) ready(ctx context.Context) map[string]string {
        out := map[string]string{"db": "ok", "corp_mcp": "ok", "redis": "ok"}
        cctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 2*time.Second)
        defer cancel()
        if err := a.Store.Ping(cctx); err != nil {
                out["db"] = err.Error()
        }
        if _, err := a.MCP.ListTools(cctx); err != nil {
                out["corp_mcp"] = err.Error()
        }
        if a.redis != nil {
                if err := a.redis.Ping(cctx).Err(); err != nil {
                        out["redis"] = err.Error()
                }
        } else {
                out["redis"] = "ok (in-memory fallback)"
        }
        return out
}

type mockAdmin struct{ m *corpmock.Mock }

func (m mockAdmin) Reset(context.Context) error               { m.m.Reset(); return nil }
func (m mockAdmin) SetFault(_ context.Context, on bool) error { m.m.SetFault(on); return nil }

// demo implements httpapi.Demo.
type demo struct {
        a     *App
        fault bool
}

func (d *demo) State(context.Context) httpapi.DemoState {
        now := d.a.Clock.Now()
        return httpapi.DemoState{Enabled: d.a.Cfg.DemoMode, Now: now, Today: route.FormatDate(now), Day: clock.DayOfInternship(directory.InternshipStart, now), CorpFault: d.fault}
}

func (d *demo) Advance(ctx context.Context, days int) error {
        if d.a.Sim == nil {
                return errors.New("simulated clock is off")
        }
        t := d.a.Sim.AdvanceDays(days)
        if err := d.a.Store.SaveClock(ctx, t, time.Now()); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return d.a.Cron.Tick(ctx)
}

func (d *demo) Reset(ctx context.Context) error {
        if err := d.a.Store.Truncate(ctx); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if d.a.Sim != nil {
                d.a.Sim.Set(d.a.Cfg.SimStart)
                if err := d.a.Store.SaveClock(ctx, d.a.Cfg.SimStart, time.Now()); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        if err := d.a.Admin.Reset(ctx); err != nil {
                d.a.Log.Warn("corp reset failed", "err", err)
        }
        d.fault = false
        d.a.MCP.Reset()
        if err := seed.Run(ctx, d.a.Store); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        if err := d.a.Mentor.RebuildIndex(ctx); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return d.a.Cron.Tick(ctx) // the intern immediately gets the morning brief of day 1
}

func (d *demo) SetCorpFault(ctx context.Context, on bool) error {
        if err := d.a.Admin.SetFault(ctx, on); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        d.fault = on
        if !on {
                d.a.MCP.Reset()
        }
        return nil
}

// Run serves HTTP and runs cron until ctx is cancelled.
func (a *App) Run(ctx context.Context) error {
        srv := &http.Server{Addr: a.Cfg.Addr, Handler: a.Handler, ReadHeaderTimeout: 10 * time.Second}
        if !a.Cfg.DisableCron {
                go a.Cron.Run(ctx, a.Cfg.CronInterval)
        }
        errc := make(chan error, 1)
        go func() {
                a.Log.Info("tropa listening", "addr", a.Cfg.Addr, "demo", a.Cfg.DemoMode, "corp", a.Cfg.CorpMCPURL == "")
                errc <- srv.ListenAndServe()
        }()
        select {
        case <-ctx.Done():
                sctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Second)
                defer cancel()
                return srv.Shutdown(sctx)
        case err := <-errc:
                return err
        }
}

// Close releases resources.
func (a *App) Close() {
        if a.redis != nil {
                _ = a.redis.Close()
        }
        a.Store.Close()
}

// NewLogger creates a JSON logger.
func NewLogger(level string) *slog.Logger {
        var l slog.Level
        _ = l.UnmarshalText([]byte(level))
        return slog.New(slog.NewJSONHandler(os.Stdout, &slog.HandlerOptions{Level: l}))
}

package app

import (
        "fmt"
        "os"
        "strconv"
        "strings"
        "time"
)

// Config is read from environment variables (see .env.example).
type Config struct {
        Addr          string
        DatabaseURL   string
        DBSchema      string
        RedisURL      string
        CorpMCPURL    string // empty → embedded in-process corporate mock
        CorpToken     string
        CorpLatency   time.Duration
        SessionSecret string
        SecureCookie  bool
        DemoMode      bool
        SimStart      time.Time
        LLMProvider   string // none | anthropic
        AnthropicKey  string
        AnthropicURL  string
        LLMModel      string
        LLMEffort     string
        LLMFallback   bool
        CronInterval  time.Duration
        LogLevel      string
        DisableCron   bool
}

func env(k, def string) string {
        if v, ok := os.LookupEnv(k); ok && v != "" {
                return v
        }
        return def
}

func envBool(k string, def bool) bool {
        v, ok := os.LookupEnv(k)
        if !ok || v == "" {
                return def
        }
        b, err := strconv.ParseBool(v)
        if err != nil {
                return def
        }
        return b
}

// LoadConfig reads configuration from the environment.
func LoadConfig() (Config, error) {
        c := Config{
                Addr:          env("HTTP_ADDR", ":8080"),
                DatabaseURL:   env("DATABASE_URL", "postgres://localhost:5432/tropa?sslmode=disable"),
                DBSchema:      env("DB_SCHEMA", ""),
                RedisURL:      env("REDIS_URL", ""),
                CorpMCPURL:    env("CORP_MCP_URL", ""),
                CorpToken:     env("CORP_TOKEN", "dev-corp-token"),
                SessionSecret: env("SESSION_SECRET", ""),
                SecureCookie:  envBool("SECURE_COOKIE", false),
                DemoMode:      envBool("DEMO_MODE", true),
                LLMProvider:   strings.ToLower(env("LLM_PROVIDER", "none")),
                AnthropicKey:  env("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", ""),
                AnthropicURL:  env("ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL", ""),
                LLMModel:      env("LLM_MODEL", "claude-opus-5-5"),
                LLMEffort:     env("LLM_EFFORT", "low"),
                LLMFallback:   envBool("LLM_SERVER_FALLBACK", true),
                LogLevel:      env("LOG_LEVEL", "info"),
                DisableCron:   envBool("DISABLE_CRON", false),
        }
        var err error
        if c.SimStart, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, env("SIM_START", "2026-11-09T09:30:00+03:00")); err != nil {
                return c, fmt.Errorf("SIM_START: %w", err)
        }
        if c.CronInterval, err = time.ParseDuration(env("CRON_INTERVAL", "20s")); err != nil {
                return c, fmt.Errorf("CRON_INTERVAL: %w", err)
        }
        if c.CorpLatency, err = time.ParseDuration(env("CORP_LATENCY", "350ms")); err != nil {
                return c, fmt.Errorf("CORP_LATENCY: %w", err)
        }
        if c.SessionSecret == "" {
                if !c.DemoMode {
                        return c, fmt.Errorf("SESSION_SECRET is required when DEMO_MODE=false")
                }
                c.SessionSecret = "demo-only-session-secret-change-me"
        }
        if c.LLMProvider == "anthropic" && c.AnthropicKey == "" {
                return c, fmt.Errorf("LLM_PROVIDER=anthropic requires ANTHROPIC_API_KEY")
        }
        return c, nil
}

// Package apperr defines typed application errors mapped to HTTP statuses.
package apperr

import (
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "net/http"
)

// Error is an application error safe to show to the user.
type Error struct {
        Status  int    `json:"-"`
        Code    string `json:"code"`
        Message string `json:"message"`
}

func (e *Error) Error() string { return e.Message }

func newf(status int, code, format string, a ...any) *Error {
        return &Error{Status: status, Code: code, Message: fmt.Sprintf(format, a...)}
}

// Forbidden — the user lacks permission.
func Forbidden(format string, a ...any) *Error {
        return newf(http.StatusForbidden, "forbidden", format, a...)
}

// NotFound — the resource does not exist (or is hidden from the user).
func NotFound(format string, a ...any) *Error {
        return newf(http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", format, a...)
}

// BadRequest — invalid input.
func BadRequest(format string, a ...any) *Error {
        return newf(http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", format, a...)
}

// Conflict — state does not allow the operation.
func Conflict(format string, a ...any) *Error {
        return newf(http.StatusConflict, "conflict", format, a...)
}

// Unavailable — a dependency is down.
func Unavailable(format string, a ...any) *Error {
        return newf(http.StatusServiceUnavailable, "unavailable", format, a...)
}

// TooMany — rate limit exceeded.
func TooMany(format string, a ...any) *Error {
        return newf(http.StatusTooManyRequests, "rate_limited", format, a...)
}

// As extracts an *Error.
func As(err error) (*Error, bool) {
        var e *Error
        ok := errors.As(err, &e)
        return e, ok
}

// Package auth imitates the existing corporate SSO: it issues a short-lived
// signed session (JWT in an HttpOnly cookie) for synthetic directory users.
package auth

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "net/http"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "github.com/golang-jwt/jwt/v5"
        "golang.org/x/crypto/bcrypt"

        "tropa/internal/directory"
)

// CookieName is the session cookie.
const CookieName = "tropa_session"

// ErrBadCredentials is returned on wrong login/password.
var ErrBadCredentials = errors.New("неверный логин или пароль")

// Auth issues and verifies sessions.
type Auth struct {
        secret       []byte
        dir          *directory.Directory
        ttl          time.Duration
        secureCookie bool
}

// New creates an authenticator.
func New(secret string, dir *directory.Directory, ttl time.Duration, secureCookie bool) *Auth {
        return &Auth{secret: []byte(secret), dir: dir, ttl: ttl, secureCookie: secureCookie}
}

// Login checks credentials and returns a signed token.
func (a *Auth) Login(login, password string) (*directory.Person, string, error) {
        p, ok := a.dir.ByLogin(strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(login)))
        if !ok {
                // constant-ish time: still run bcrypt to avoid user enumeration by timing
                _ = bcrypt.CompareHashAndPassword([]byte("$2a$10$Dv/RbCZ1pksVewvkQBhIBevjBZorRvAThvEBbX8Xe.TQuxLoTFT7u"), []byte(password+"x"))
                return nil, "", ErrBadCredentials
        }
        if bcrypt.CompareHashAndPassword([]byte(p.PasswordHash()), []byte(password)) != nil {
                return nil, "", ErrBadCredentials
        }
        tok, err := a.Issue(p.ID)
        return p, tok, err
}

// Issue signs a session token for a user id.
func (a *Auth) Issue(userID string) (string, error) {
        now := time.Now()
        claims := jwt.RegisteredClaims{Subject: userID, IssuedAt: jwt.NewNumericDate(now), ExpiresAt: jwt.NewNumericDate(now.Add(a.ttl)), Issuer: "tropa"}
        return jwt.NewWithClaims(jwt.SigningMethodHS256, claims).SignedString(a.secret)
}

// Verify parses a token and returns the user.
func (a *Auth) Verify(tok string) (*directory.Person, error) {
        claims := &jwt.RegisteredClaims{}
        _, err := jwt.ParseWithClaims(tok, claims, func(t *jwt.Token) (any, error) { return a.secret, nil },
                jwt.WithValidMethods([]string{jwt.SigningMethodHS256.Alg()}), jwt.WithIssuer("tropa"))
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        p, ok := a.dir.ByID(claims.Subject)
        if !ok {
                return nil, errors.New("unknown user")
        }
        return p, nil
}

// SetCookie writes the session cookie.
func (a *Auth) SetCookie(w http.ResponseWriter, tok string) {
        http.SetCookie(w, &http.Cookie{Name: CookieName, Value: tok, Path: "/", HttpOnly: true, Secure: a.secureCookie, SameSite: http.SameSiteStrictMode, MaxAge: int(a.ttl.Seconds())})
}

// ClearCookie removes the session cookie.
func (a *Auth) ClearCookie(w http.ResponseWriter) {
        http.SetCookie(w, &http.Cookie{Name: CookieName, Value: "", Path: "/", HttpOnly: true, Secure: a.secureCookie, SameSite: http.SameSiteStrictMode, MaxAge: -1})
}

type ctxKey struct{}

// WithUser stores the user in a context.
func WithUser(ctx context.Context, p *directory.Person) context.Context {
        return context.WithValue(ctx, ctxKey{}, p)
}

// UserFrom returns the authenticated user (nil if none).
func UserFrom(ctx context.Context) *directory.Person {
        p, _ := ctx.Value(ctxKey{}).(*directory.Person)
        return p
}

// FromRequest resolves the user from the cookie or a Bearer token.
func (a *Auth) FromRequest(r *http.Request) (*directory.Person, error) {
        tok := ""
        if c, err := r.Cookie(CookieName); err == nil {
                tok = c.Value
        }
        if h := r.Header.Get("Authorization"); strings.HasPrefix(h, "Bearer ") {
                tok = strings.TrimPrefix(h, "Bearer ")
        }
        if tok == "" {
                return nil, errors.New("no session")
        }
        return a.Verify(tok)
}

// Package catalog loads and validates the embedded onboarding content:
// routes, team packs, knowledge-base documents, access catalog and rubrics.
package catalog

import (
        "bytes"
        "fmt"
        "io/fs"
        "path"
        "sort"
        "strings"

        "gopkg.in/yaml.v3"
)

// Task is one onboarding step in a route template.
type Task struct {
        ID           string   `yaml:"id" json:"id"`
        Title        string   `yaml:"title" json:"title"`
        Owner        string   `yaml:"owner" json:"owner"` // intern | mentor | lead
        Kind         string   `yaml:"kind" json:"kind"`   // manual | access | teachback
        DueDay       int      `yaml:"due_day" json:"due_day"`
        DependsOn    []string `yaml:"depends_on" json:"depends_on"`
        Systems      []string `yaml:"systems" json:"systems,omitempty"`
        Topic        string   `yaml:"topic" json:"topic,omitempty"`
        Doc          string   `yaml:"doc" json:"doc,omitempty"`
        ChecklistRef string   `yaml:"checklist_ref" json:"checklist_ref,omitempty"`
}

// Stage groups tasks of a period.
type Stage struct {
        ID      string `yaml:"id" json:"id"`
        Title   string `yaml:"title" json:"title"`
        FromDay int    `yaml:"from_day" json:"from_day"`
        ToDay   int    `yaml:"to_day" json:"to_day"`
        Tasks   []Task `yaml:"tasks" json:"tasks"`
}

// Route is an onboarding route template for a track.
type Route struct {
        ID     string  `yaml:"id"`
        Title  string  `yaml:"title"`
        Stages []Stage `yaml:"stages"`
}

// Task returns a task by id.
func (r *Route) Task(id string) (Task, bool) {
        for _, s := range r.Stages {
                for _, t := range s.Tasks {
                        if t.ID == id {
                                return t, true
                        }
                }
        }
        return Task{}, false
}

// AllTasks returns tasks in route order.
func (r *Route) AllTasks() []Task {
        var out []Task
        for _, s := range r.Stages {
                out = append(out, s.Tasks...)
        }
        return out
}

// Contact is a "who to ask" entry of a team pack.
type Contact struct {
        Topic    string   `yaml:"topic"`
        Keywords []string `yaml:"keywords"`
        Person   string   `yaml:"person"`
        Channel  string   `yaml:"channel"`
        How      string   `yaml:"how"`
}

// CalendarEvent is a team event used for absence conflict checks.
type CalendarEvent struct {
        Date       string `yaml:"date" json:"date"`
        Time       string `yaml:"time" json:"time"`
        Title      string `yaml:"title" json:"title"`
        Importance string `yaml:"importance" json:"importance"`
        Note       string `yaml:"note" json:"note"`
}

// Team is a team pack: onboarding-as-code maintained by the team itself.
type Team struct {
        ID       string          `yaml:"id"`
        Name     string          `yaml:"name"`
        Route    string          `yaml:"route"`
        Contacts []Contact       `yaml:"contacts"`
        Calendar []CalendarEvent `yaml:"calendar"`
}

// AccessSystem is an entry of the access-service catalog.
type AccessSystem struct {
        ID           string   `yaml:"id" json:"id"`
        Title        string   `yaml:"title" json:"title"`
        Level        string   `yaml:"level" json:"level"`
        Approval     string   `yaml:"approval" json:"approval"` // auto | lead
        Standard     bool     `yaml:"standard" json:"standard"`
        ForbiddenFor []string `yaml:"forbidden_for" json:"forbidden_for,omitempty"`
        Aliases      []string `yaml:"aliases" json:"-"`
}

// Concept is one rubric point of a teach-back.
type Concept struct {
        ID       string   `yaml:"id" json:"id"`
        Label    string   `yaml:"label" json:"label"`
        Keywords []string `yaml:"keywords" json:"-"`
        Hint     string   `yaml:"hint" json:"hint"`
}

// Rubric describes how to grade a teach-back ("Расскажи о …").
type Rubric struct {
        Topic     string    `yaml:"topic"`
        Title     string    `yaml:"title"`
        Doc       string    `yaml:"doc"`
        PassRatio float64   `yaml:"pass_ratio"`
        Prompt    string    `yaml:"prompt"`
        Concepts  []Concept `yaml:"concepts"`
}

// Section is a heading-delimited chunk of a document.
type Section struct {
        Heading string
        Text    string
}

// Doc is a knowledge-base document (test instruction) with its ACL.
type Doc struct {
        ID         string   `yaml:"id"`
        Title      string   `yaml:"title"`
        Visibility string   `yaml:"visibility"` // public | team | restricted
        Teams      []string `yaml:"teams"`
        Roles      []string `yaml:"roles"`
        Tags       []string `yaml:"tags"`
        Sections   []Section
}

// Catalog is the whole validated content set.
type Catalog struct {
        Routes   map[string]*Route
        Teams    map[string]*Team
        Docs     map[string]*Doc
        Access   []AccessSystem
        Rubrics  map[string]*Rubric
        DocOrder []string
}

// AccessSystem returns a catalog entry by id.
func (c *Catalog) AccessSystem(id string) (AccessSystem, bool) {
        for _, s := range c.Access {
                if s.ID == id {
                        return s, true
                }
        }
        return AccessSystem{}, false
}

// Load parses and validates content from fsys.
func Load(fsys fs.FS) (*Catalog, error) {
        c := &Catalog{Routes: map[string]*Route{}, Teams: map[string]*Team{}, Docs: map[string]*Doc{}, Rubrics: map[string]*Rubric{}}
        if err := eachFile(fsys, "routes", ".yaml", func(b []byte, name string) error {
                var r Route
                if err := yaml.Unmarshal(b, &r); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
                c.Routes[r.ID] = &r
                return nil
        }); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if err := eachFile(fsys, "teams", ".yaml", func(b []byte, name string) error {
                var t Team
                if err := yaml.Unmarshal(b, &t); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
                c.Teams[t.ID] = &t
                return nil
        }); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if err := eachFile(fsys, "teachback", ".yaml", func(b []byte, name string) error {
                var r Rubric
                if err := yaml.Unmarshal(b, &r); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
                c.Rubrics[r.Topic] = &r
                return nil
        }); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if err := eachFile(fsys, "kb", ".md", func(b []byte, name string) error {
                d, err := ParseDoc(b)
                if err != nil {
                        return fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", name, err)
                }
                c.Docs[d.ID] = d
                c.DocOrder = append(c.DocOrder, d.ID)
                return nil
        }); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        b, err := fs.ReadFile(fsys, "access_catalog.yaml")
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        var ac struct {
                Systems []AccessSystem `yaml:"systems"`
        }
        if err := yaml.Unmarshal(b, &ac); err != nil {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("access_catalog: %w", err)
        }
        c.Access = ac.Systems
        sort.Strings(c.DocOrder)
        return c, c.Validate()
}

func eachFile(fsys fs.FS, dir, ext string, fn func([]byte, string) error) error {
        entries, err := fs.ReadDir(fsys, dir)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        for _, e := range entries {
                if e.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(e.Name(), ext) {
                        continue
                }
                p := path.Join(dir, e.Name())
                b, err := fs.ReadFile(fsys, p)
                if err != nil {
                        return err
                }
                if err := fn(b, p); err != nil {
                        return fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", p, err)
                }
        }
        return nil
}

// ParseDoc parses a markdown document with YAML front matter.
func ParseDoc(b []byte) (*Doc, error) {
        b = bytes.ReplaceAll(b, []byte("\r\n"), []byte("\n"))
        if !bytes.HasPrefix(b, []byte("---\n")) {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("missing front matter")
        }
        rest := b[4:]
        end := bytes.Index(rest, []byte("\n---\n"))
        if end < 0 {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("unterminated front matter")
        }
        var d Doc
        if err := yaml.Unmarshal(rest[:end], &d); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if d.ID == "" || d.Title == "" {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("doc needs id and title")
        }
        if d.Visibility == "" {
                d.Visibility = "public"
        }
        body := string(rest[end+5:])
        var cur *Section
        for _, line := range strings.Split(body, "\n") {
                if strings.HasPrefix(line, "## ") {
                        d.Sections = append(d.Sections, Section{Heading: strings.TrimSpace(line[3:])})
                        cur = &d.Sections[len(d.Sections)-1]
                        continue
                }
                if cur == nil {
                        if strings.TrimSpace(line) == "" {
                                continue
                        }
                        d.Sections = append(d.Sections, Section{Heading: d.Title})
                        cur = &d.Sections[len(d.Sections)-1]
                }
                cur.Text += line + "\n"
        }
        for i := range d.Sections {
                d.Sections[i].Text = strings.TrimSpace(d.Sections[i].Text)
        }
        return &d, nil
}

// Validate checks referential integrity of the content.
func (c *Catalog) Validate() error {
        var errs []string
        for _, r := range c.Routes {
                seen := map[string]bool{}
                for _, t := range r.AllTasks() {
                        if seen[t.ID] {
                                errs = append(errs, fmt.Sprintf("route %s: duplicate task %s", r.ID, t.ID))
                        }
                        seen[t.ID] = true
                        switch t.Owner {
                        case "intern", "mentor", "lead":
                        default:
                                errs = append(errs, fmt.Sprintf("route %s: task %s bad owner %q", r.ID, t.ID, t.Owner))
                        }
                        if t.Doc != "" && c.Docs[t.Doc] == nil {
                                errs = append(errs, fmt.Sprintf("route %s: task %s unknown doc %s", r.ID, t.ID, t.Doc))
                        }
                        if t.Kind == "teachback" && c.Rubrics[t.Topic] == nil {
                                errs = append(errs, fmt.Sprintf("route %s: task %s unknown rubric %s", r.ID, t.ID, t.Topic))
                        }
                        for _, s := range t.Systems {
                                if _, ok := c.AccessSystem(s); !ok {
                                        errs = append(errs, fmt.Sprintf("route %s: task %s unknown system %s", r.ID, t.ID, s))
                                }
                        }
                }
                for _, t := range r.AllTasks() {
                        for _, d := range t.DependsOn {
                                if !seen[d] {
                                        errs = append(errs, fmt.Sprintf("route %s: task %s depends on unknown %s", r.ID, t.ID, d))
                                }
                        }
                }
        }
        for _, t := range c.Teams {
                if c.Routes[t.Route] == nil {
                        errs = append(errs, fmt.Sprintf("team %s: unknown route %s", t.ID, t.Route))
                }
        }
        for _, r := range c.Rubrics {
                if c.Docs[r.Doc] == nil {
                        errs = append(errs, fmt.Sprintf("rubric %s: unknown doc %s", r.Topic, r.Doc))
                }
        }
        if len(errs) > 0 {
                sort.Strings(errs)
                return fmt.Errorf("content invalid: %s", strings.Join(errs, "; "))
        }
        return nil
}

// Package clock provides the time source. In demo mode a simulated clock lets
// reviewers "time-travel" through the first weeks of an internship; all
// domain logic reads time only through Clock, which also makes it testable.
package clock

import (
        "sync"
        "time"
)

// MSK is the timezone used for the synthetic company.
var MSK = time.FixedZone("MSK", 3*60*60)

// Clock is the single time source of the application.
type Clock interface{ Now() time.Time }

// Real is the wall clock.
type Real struct{}

// Now returns the current wall-clock time in MSK.
func (Real) Now() time.Time { return time.Now().In(MSK) }

// Sim is a simulated clock: Now = base + (wall - setAt). Time keeps flowing
// naturally inside a simulated day, and Advance jumps to the next morning.
type Sim struct {
        mu    sync.RWMutex
        base  time.Time
        setAt time.Time
        wall  func() time.Time
}

// NewSim creates a simulated clock anchored at base.
func NewSim(base time.Time, wall func() time.Time) *Sim {
        if wall == nil {
                wall = time.Now
        }
        return &Sim{base: base.In(MSK), setAt: wall(), wall: wall}
}

// Now returns simulated time.
func (s *Sim) Now() time.Time {
        s.mu.RLock()
        defer s.mu.RUnlock()
        return s.base.Add(s.wall().Sub(s.setAt)).In(MSK)
}

// Set moves the simulated time to t.
func (s *Sim) Set(t time.Time) {
        s.mu.Lock()
        defer s.mu.Unlock()
        s.base = t.In(MSK)
        s.setAt = s.wall()
}

// AdvanceDays jumps n days ahead to 09:30 MSK of the target day.
func (s *Sim) AdvanceDays(n int) time.Time {
        d := StartOfDay(s.Now()).AddDate(0, 0, n).Add(9*time.Hour + 30*time.Minute)
        s.Set(d)
        return d
}

// StartOfDay truncates t to midnight MSK.
func StartOfDay(t time.Time) time.Time {
        t = t.In(MSK)
        return time.Date(t.Year(), t.Month(), t.Day(), 0, 0, 0, 0, MSK)
}

// DayOfInternship returns 1-based calendar day number since start.
func DayOfInternship(start, now time.Time) int {
        d := int(StartOfDay(now).Sub(StartOfDay(start)).Hours()/24) + 1
        if d < 1 {
                return 1
        }
        return d
}

// AddWorkdays adds n working days (Mon–Fri) to t's date.
func AddWorkdays(t time.Time, n int) time.Time {
        d := StartOfDay(t)
        for n > 0 {
                d = d.AddDate(0, 0, 1)
                if d.Weekday() != time.Saturday && d.Weekday() != time.Sunday {
                        n--
                }
        }
        return d
}

// WorkdaysBetween counts working days in (from, to].
func WorkdaysBetween(from, to time.Time) int {
        a, b := StartOfDay(from), StartOfDay(to)
        n := 0
        for d := a.AddDate(0, 0, 1); !d.After(b); d = d.AddDate(0, 0, 1) {
                if d.Weekday() != time.Saturday && d.Weekday() != time.Sunday {
                        n++
                }
        }
        return n
}

package corp

import (
        "bytes"
        "context"
        "encoding/json"
        "fmt"
        "net/http"
        "strings"
        "time"
)

// Admin controls the corporate mock (demo only): reset and outage simulation.
type Admin interface {
        Reset(ctx context.Context) error
        SetFault(ctx context.Context, on bool) error
}

// HTTPAdmin talks to a remote corp-mcp /admin API.
type HTTPAdmin struct {
        BaseURL string
        Token   string
        HTTP    *http.Client
}

// NewHTTPAdmin derives the admin base URL from the MCP URL (…/mcp → …).
func NewHTTPAdmin(mcpURL, token string) *HTTPAdmin {
        return &HTTPAdmin{BaseURL: strings.TrimSuffix(mcpURL, "/mcp"), Token: token, HTTP: &http.Client{Timeout: 5 * time.Second}}
}

func (a *HTTPAdmin) post(ctx context.Context, path string, body any) error {
        b, _ := json.Marshal(body)
        req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, a.BaseURL+path, bytes.NewReader(b))
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+a.Token)
        req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
        resp, err := a.HTTP.Do(req)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        defer resp.Body.Close()
        if resp.StatusCode >= 300 {
                return fmt.Errorf("corp admin %s: http %d", path, resp.StatusCode)
        }
        return nil
}

// Reset clears the mock state.
func (a *HTTPAdmin) Reset(ctx context.Context) error {
        return a.post(ctx, "/admin/reset", map[string]any{})
}

// SetFault toggles a simulated outage.
func (a *HTTPAdmin) SetFault(ctx context.Context, on bool) error {
        return a.post(ctx, "/admin/fault", map[string]bool{"enabled": on})
}

// Package corp is the typed facade over corporate services reached via MCP.
// The agent depends on the Services interface, so tests can inject fakes and
// production can point the MCP client at real Avito connectors.
package corp

import (
        "context"

        "tropa/internal/catalog"
        "tropa/internal/mcp"
)

// Tool names exposed by the corporate MCP server.
const (
        ToolAbsenceCreate     = "absence_create_request"
        ToolAbsenceGet        = "absence_get_request"
        ToolAbsenceDecide     = "absence_decide"
        ToolCalendarEvents    = "calendar_team_events"
        ToolAccessCatalog     = "access_catalog"
        ToolAccessCreate      = "access_create_request"
        ToolAccessGet         = "access_get_request"
        ToolAccessDecide      = "access_decide"
        ToolChecklistComplete = "checklist_complete_item"
        ToolChecklistGet      = "checklist_get"
)

// AbsenceRequest is a request in the absence service.
type AbsenceRequest struct {
        RequestID       string `json:"request_id"`
        EmployeeID      string `json:"employee_id"`
        Type            string `json:"type"`
        DateFrom        string `json:"date_from"`
        DateTo          string `json:"date_to"`
        Comment         string `json:"comment"`
        Status          string `json:"status"`
        ApproverID      string `json:"approver_id"`
        ApproverName    string `json:"approver_name"`
        DecisionComment string `json:"decision_comment,omitempty"`
        CreatedAt       string `json:"created_at"`
}

// AbsenceCreate are arguments of absence_create_request.
type AbsenceCreate struct {
        EmployeeID     string `json:"employee_id"`
        Type           string `json:"type"`
        DateFrom       string `json:"date_from"`
        DateTo         string `json:"date_to"`
        Comment        string `json:"comment"`
        IdempotencyKey string `json:"idempotency_key"`
}

// TeamEvents is the calendar response used for conflict checks.
type TeamEvents struct {
        Events       []catalog.CalendarEvent `json:"events"`
        AbsentByDate map[string]int          `json:"absent_by_date"`
}

// AccessRequest is a request in the access service.
type AccessRequest struct {
        RequestID    string `json:"request_id"`
        EmployeeID   string `json:"employee_id"`
        System       string `json:"system"`
        Level        string `json:"level"`
        Status       string `json:"status"`
        ApproverID   string `json:"approver_id,omitempty"`
        ApproverName string `json:"approver_name,omitempty"`
        CreatedAt    string `json:"created_at"`
}

// AccessCreate are arguments of access_create_request.
type AccessCreate struct {
        EmployeeID     string `json:"employee_id"`
        System         string `json:"system"`
        Justification  string `json:"justification"`
        IdempotencyKey string `json:"idempotency_key"`
}

// ChecklistItem is an item of the newcomer checklist service.
type ChecklistItem struct {
        EmployeeID  string `json:"employee_id"`
        ItemID      string `json:"item_id"`
        Status      string `json:"status"`
        Evidence    string `json:"evidence,omitempty"`
        CompletedBy string `json:"completed_by,omitempty"`
        CompletedAt string `json:"completed_at,omitempty"`
}

// ChecklistComplete are arguments of checklist_complete_item.
type ChecklistComplete struct {
        EmployeeID     string `json:"employee_id"`
        ItemID         string `json:"item_id"`
        Evidence       string `json:"evidence"`
        IdempotencyKey string `json:"idempotency_key"`
}

// Services is what the buddy needs from corporate systems.
type Services interface {
        CreateAbsence(ctx context.Context, actor string, a AbsenceCreate) (AbsenceRequest, error)
        GetAbsence(ctx context.Context, actor, id string) (AbsenceRequest, error)
        DecideAbsence(ctx context.Context, actor, id string, approve bool, comment string) (AbsenceRequest, error)
        TeamEvents(ctx context.Context, actor, teamID, from, to string) (TeamEvents, error)
        CreateAccess(ctx context.Context, actor string, a AccessCreate) (AccessRequest, error)
        GetAccess(ctx context.Context, actor, id string) (AccessRequest, error)
        DecideAccess(ctx context.Context, actor, id string, approve bool, comment string) (AccessRequest, error)
        CompleteChecklist(ctx context.Context, actor string, c ChecklistComplete) (ChecklistItem, error)
}

// Client implements Services over MCP.
type Client struct{ MCP *mcp.Client }

// CreateAbsence creates a day-off request.
func (c *Client) CreateAbsence(ctx context.Context, actor string, a AbsenceCreate) (r AbsenceRequest, err error) {
        return r, c.MCP.Call(ctx, actor, ToolAbsenceCreate, a, &r)
}

// GetAbsence reads a request.
func (c *Client) GetAbsence(ctx context.Context, actor, id string) (r AbsenceRequest, err error) {
        return r, c.MCP.Call(ctx, actor, ToolAbsenceGet, map[string]string{"request_id": id}, &r)
}

// DecideAbsence approves or rejects a request (lead only).
func (c *Client) DecideAbsence(ctx context.Context, actor, id string, approve bool, comment string) (r AbsenceRequest, err error) {
        return r, c.MCP.Call(ctx, actor, ToolAbsenceDecide, map[string]any{"request_id": id, "decision": decision(approve), "comment": comment}, &r)
}

// TeamEvents returns team calendar events in a range.
func (c *Client) TeamEvents(ctx context.Context, actor, teamID, from, to string) (r TeamEvents, err error) {
        return r, c.MCP.Call(ctx, actor, ToolCalendarEvents, map[string]string{"team_id": teamID, "date_from": from, "date_to": to}, &r)
}

// CreateAccess requests access to a system.
func (c *Client) CreateAccess(ctx context.Context, actor string, a AccessCreate) (r AccessRequest, err error) {
        return r, c.MCP.Call(ctx, actor, ToolAccessCreate, a, &r)
}

// GetAccess reads an access request.
func (c *Client) GetAccess(ctx context.Context, actor, id string) (r AccessRequest, err error) {
        return r, c.MCP.Call(ctx, actor, ToolAccessGet, map[string]string{"request_id": id}, &r)
}

// DecideAccess approves or rejects an access request (lead only).
func (c *Client) DecideAccess(ctx context.Context, actor, id string, approve bool, comment string) (r AccessRequest, err error) {
        return r, c.MCP.Call(ctx, actor, ToolAccessDecide, map[string]any{"request_id": id, "decision": decision(approve), "comment": comment}, &r)
}

// CompleteChecklist marks a checklist item done.
func (c *Client) CompleteChecklist(ctx context.Context, actor string, cc ChecklistComplete) (r ChecklistItem, err error) {
        return r, c.MCP.Call(ctx, actor, ToolChecklistComplete, cc, &r)
}

func decision(approve bool) string {
        if approve {
                return "approve"
        }
        return "reject"
}

// Package corpmock imitates Avito corporate services (absences, access,
// newcomer checklist, team calendar) behind an MCP server. It enforces its own
// permissions on the delegated identity (X-On-Behalf-Of) — defence in depth:
// even a buggy agent cannot approve its own request.
package corpmock

import (
        "context"
        "encoding/json"
        "fmt"
        "net/http"
        "os"
        "slices"
        "sync"
        "time"

        "tropa/internal/catalog"
        "tropa/internal/clock"
        "tropa/internal/corp"
        "tropa/internal/directory"
        "tropa/internal/mcp"
)

type state struct {
        Seq       int                                       `json:"seq"`
        Absences  map[string]*corp.AbsenceRequest           `json:"absences"`
        Access    map[string]*corp.AccessRequest            `json:"access"`
        Checklist map[string]map[string]*corp.ChecklistItem `json:"checklist"`
        Idem      map[string]string                         `json:"idem"`
}

func newState() state {
        return state{Absences: map[string]*corp.AbsenceRequest{}, Access: map[string]*corp.AccessRequest{},
                Checklist: map[string]map[string]*corp.ChecklistItem{}, Idem: map[string]string{}}
}

// Mock is the corporate services imitation.
type Mock struct {
        mu        sync.Mutex
        st        state
        dir       *directory.Directory
        cat       *catalog.Catalog
        now       func() time.Time
        fault     bool
        Latency   time.Duration
        StatePath string
        Server    *mcp.Server
}

// New creates the mock and registers its MCP tools.
func New(dir *directory.Directory, cat *catalog.Catalog, token string, now func() time.Time) *Mock {
        if now == nil {
                now = func() time.Time { return time.Now().In(clock.MSK) }
        }
        m := &Mock{st: newState(), dir: dir, cat: cat, now: now}
        m.Server = mcp.NewServer("avito-corp-services-mock", "1.0.0", token)
        m.Server.Before = m.before
        m.register()
        return m
}

func (m *Mock) before(ctx context.Context) error {
        m.mu.Lock()
        fault, lat := m.fault, m.Latency
        m.mu.Unlock()
        if fault {
                return mcp.ErrUnavailable
        }
        if lat > 0 {
                select {
                case <-ctx.Done():
                        return ctx.Err()
                case <-time.After(lat):
                }
        }
        return nil
}

// SetFault toggles a simulated outage.
func (m *Mock) SetFault(on bool) { m.mu.Lock(); m.fault = on; m.mu.Unlock() }

// Fault reports the outage flag.
func (m *Mock) Fault() bool { m.mu.Lock(); defer m.mu.Unlock(); return m.fault }

// Reset clears all state.
func (m *Mock) Reset() {
        m.mu.Lock()
        m.st, m.fault = newState(), false
        m.mu.Unlock()
        m.save()
}

// Load restores state from StatePath if present.
func (m *Mock) Load() error {
        if m.StatePath == "" {
                return nil
        }
        b, err := os.ReadFile(m.StatePath)
        if os.IsNotExist(err) {
                return nil
        }
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        st := newState()
        if err := json.Unmarshal(b, &st); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        m.mu.Lock()
        m.st = st
        m.mu.Unlock()
        return nil
}

func (m *Mock) save() {
        if m.StatePath == "" {
                return
        }
        m.mu.Lock()
        b, _ := json.Marshal(m.st)
        m.mu.Unlock()
        _ = os.WriteFile(m.StatePath, b, 0o600)
}

func (m *Mock) nextID(prefix string) string {
        m.st.Seq++
        return fmt.Sprintf("%s-%05d", prefix, m.st.Seq)
}

func obj(props map[string]any, required ...string) map[string]any {
        return map[string]any{"type": "object", "properties": props, "required": required, "additionalProperties": false}
}

func str(desc string) map[string]any { return map[string]any{"type": "string", "description": desc} }

func decode[T any](raw json.RawMessage) (T, error) {
        var v T
        dec := json.NewDecoder(bytesReader(raw))
        dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
        if err := dec.Decode(&v); err != nil {
                return v, mcp.Errorf("invalid_arguments", "некорректные аргументы: %v", err)
        }
        return v, nil
}

func (m *Mock) person(id string) (*directory.Person, error) {
        p, ok := m.dir.ByID(id)
        if !ok {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("unknown_employee", "сотрудник %s не найден", id)
        }
        return p, nil
}

func (m *Mock) register() {
        write := mcp.Annotations{ReadOnlyHint: false, IdempotentHint: true}
        read := mcp.Annotations{ReadOnlyHint: true, IdempotentHint: true}
        m.Server.Register(mcp.Tool{Info: mcp.ToolInfo{Name: corp.ToolAbsenceCreate, Title: "Создать заявку на отгул",
                Description: "Создаёт заявку на отгул в сервисе отсутствий от имени сотрудника. Требует подтверждения пользователя.",
                InputSchema: obj(map[string]any{"employee_id": str("ID сотрудника"), "type": map[string]any{"type": "string", "enum": []string{"day_off"}},
                        "date_from": str("YYYY-MM-DD"), "date_to": str("YYYY-MM-DD"), "comment": str("Комментарий"), "idempotency_key": str("Ключ идемпотентности")},
                        "employee_id", "type", "date_from", "date_to", "idempotency_key"), Annotations: write}, Handler: m.absenceCreate})
        m.Server.Register(mcp.Tool{Info: mcp.ToolInfo{Name: corp.ToolAbsenceGet, Title: "Статус заявки на отгул", Description: "Возвращает заявку на отгул.",
                InputSchema: obj(map[string]any{"request_id": str("ID заявки")}, "request_id"), Annotations: read}, Handler: m.absenceGet})
        m.Server.Register(mcp.Tool{Info: mcp.ToolInfo{Name: corp.ToolAbsenceDecide, Title: "Согласовать отгул", Description: "Согласует или отклоняет заявку (только руководитель команды).",
                InputSchema: obj(map[string]any{"request_id": str("ID заявки"), "decision": map[string]any{"type": "string", "enum": []string{"approve", "reject"}}, "comment": str("Комментарий")}, "request_id", "decision"),
                Annotations: mcp.Annotations{ReadOnlyHint: false, DestructiveHint: false}}, Handler: m.absenceDecide})
        m.Server.Register(mcp.Tool{Info: mcp.ToolInfo{Name: corp.ToolCalendarEvents, Title: "Календарь команды", Description: "События команды и число отсутствующих по датам (без имён).",
                InputSchema: obj(map[string]any{"team_id": str("ID команды"), "date_from": str("YYYY-MM-DD"), "date_to": str("YYYY-MM-DD")}, "team_id", "date_from", "date_to"), Annotations: read}, Handler: m.calendar})
        m.Server.Register(mcp.Tool{Info: mcp.ToolInfo{Name: corp.ToolAccessCatalog, Title: "Каталог доступов", Description: "Системы, уровни и порядок согласования.",
                InputSchema: obj(map[string]any{}), Annotations: read}, Handler: m.accessCatalog})
        m.Server.Register(mcp.Tool{Info: mcp.ToolInfo{Name: corp.ToolAccessCreate, Title: "Запросить доступ", Description: "Создаёт заявку на доступ. Стандартные выдаются сразу, остальные — после согласования руководителем.",
                InputSchema: obj(map[string]any{"employee_id": str("ID сотрудника"), "system": str("ID системы из каталога"), "justification": str("Обоснование"), "idempotency_key": str("Ключ идемпотентности")},
                        "employee_id", "system", "idempotency_key"), Annotations: write}, Handler: m.accessCreate})
        m.Server.Register(mcp.Tool{Info: mcp.ToolInfo{Name: corp.ToolAccessGet, Title: "Статус заявки на доступ", Description: "Возвращает заявку на доступ.",
                InputSchema: obj(map[string]any{"request_id": str("ID заявки")}, "request_id"), Annotations: read}, Handler: m.accessGet})
        m.Server.Register(mcp.Tool{Info: mcp.ToolInfo{Name: corp.ToolAccessDecide, Title: "Согласовать доступ", Description: "Согласует или отклоняет доступ (только руководитель команды).",
                InputSchema: obj(map[string]any{"request_id": str("ID заявки"), "decision": map[string]any{"type": "string", "enum": []string{"approve", "reject"}}, "comment": str("Комментарий")}, "request_id", "decision"),
                Annotations: mcp.Annotations{ReadOnlyHint: false}}, Handler: m.accessDecide})
        m.Server.Register(mcp.Tool{Info: mcp.ToolInfo{Name: corp.ToolChecklistComplete, Title: "Отметить пункт чек-листа", Description: "Отмечает пункт чек-листа новичка выполненным.",
                InputSchema: obj(map[string]any{"employee_id": str("ID стажёра"), "item_id": str("ID пункта"), "evidence": str("Подтверждение"), "idempotency_key": str("Ключ идемпотентности")},
                        "employee_id", "item_id", "idempotency_key"), Annotations: write}, Handler: m.checklistComplete})
        m.Server.Register(mcp.Tool{Info: mcp.ToolInfo{Name: corp.ToolChecklistGet, Title: "Чек-лист новичка", Description: "Пункты чек-листа сотрудника.",
                InputSchema: obj(map[string]any{"employee_id": str("ID стажёра")}, "employee_id"), Annotations: read}, Handler: m.checklistGet})
}

func parseDate(s string) (time.Time, error) {
        t, err := time.ParseInLocation("2006-01-02", s, clock.MSK)
        if err != nil {
                return t, mcp.Errorf("invalid_date", "некорректная дата %q", s)
        }
        return t, nil
}

func (m *Mock) absenceCreate(_ context.Context, cc mcp.CallContext, raw json.RawMessage) (any, error) {
        a, err := decode[corp.AbsenceCreate](raw)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if cc.OnBehalfOf != a.EmployeeID {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("forbidden", "заявку на отгул можно подать только за себя")
        }
        emp, err := m.person(a.EmployeeID)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        from, err := parseDate(a.DateFrom)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        to, err := parseDate(a.DateTo)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if a.Type != "day_off" {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("unsupported_type", "поддерживается только тип day_off")
        }
        if to.Before(from) {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("invalid_range", "дата окончания раньше даты начала")
        }
        if to.Sub(from) > 2*24*time.Hour {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("too_long", "отгул оформляется максимум на 3 дня подряд (тестовое правило)")
        }
        if from.Before(clock.StartOfDay(m.now())) {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("in_past", "нельзя оформить отгул задним числом")
        }
        m.mu.Lock()
        if id, ok := m.st.Idem[a.IdempotencyKey]; ok && a.IdempotencyKey != "" {
                r := *m.st.Absences[id]
                m.mu.Unlock()
                return r, nil
        }
        lead, _ := m.dir.LeadOf(emp.TeamID)
        r := &corp.AbsenceRequest{RequestID: m.nextID("ABS"), EmployeeID: emp.ID, Type: a.Type, DateFrom: a.DateFrom, DateTo: a.DateTo, Comment: a.Comment,
                Status: "pending_approval", CreatedAt: m.now().Format(time.RFC3339)}
        if lead != nil {
                r.ApproverID, r.ApproverName = lead.ID, lead.Name
        }
        m.st.Absences[r.RequestID] = r
        m.st.Idem[a.IdempotencyKey] = r.RequestID
        out := *r
        m.mu.Unlock()
        m.save()
        return out, nil
}

func (m *Mock) canRead(actor, owner, approver string) bool {
        if actor == owner || actor == approver {
                return true
        }
        p, ok := m.dir.ByID(actor)
        return ok && p.Role == directory.RoleHRBP
}

func (m *Mock) absenceGet(_ context.Context, cc mcp.CallContext, raw json.RawMessage) (any, error) {
        a, err := decode[struct {
                RequestID string `json:"request_id"`
        }](raw)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        m.mu.Lock()
        defer m.mu.Unlock()
        r, ok := m.st.Absences[a.RequestID]
        if !ok {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("not_found", "заявка %s не найдена", a.RequestID)
        }
        if !m.canRead(cc.OnBehalfOf, r.EmployeeID, r.ApproverID) {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("forbidden", "нет прав на просмотр заявки")
        }
        return *r, nil
}

type decideArgs struct {
        RequestID string `json:"request_id"`
        Decision  string `json:"decision"`
        Comment   string `json:"comment"`
}

func (m *Mock) checkLead(actor, employeeID string) error {
        p, ok := m.dir.ByID(actor)
        e, ok2 := m.dir.ByID(employeeID)
        if !ok || !ok2 || p.Role != directory.RoleLead || p.TeamID != e.TeamID || p.ID == e.ID {
                return mcp.Errorf("forbidden", "согласовать может только руководитель команды сотрудника")
        }
        return nil
}

func (m *Mock) absenceDecide(_ context.Context, cc mcp.CallContext, raw json.RawMessage) (any, error) {
        a, err := decode[decideArgs](raw)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        m.mu.Lock()
        r, ok := m.st.Absences[a.RequestID]
        if !ok {
                m.mu.Unlock()
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("not_found", "заявка %s не найдена", a.RequestID)
        }
        if err := m.checkLead(cc.OnBehalfOf, r.EmployeeID); err != nil {
                m.mu.Unlock()
                return nil, err
        }
        if r.Status != "pending_approval" {
                out := *r
                m.mu.Unlock()
                return out, nil // idempotent: decision already made
        }
        switch a.Decision {
        case "approve":
                r.Status = "approved"
        case "reject":
                r.Status = "rejected"
        default:
                m.mu.Unlock()
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("invalid_arguments", "decision должен быть approve или reject")
        }
        r.DecisionComment = a.Comment
        out := *r
        m.mu.Unlock()
        m.save()
        return out, nil
}

func (m *Mock) calendar(_ context.Context, cc mcp.CallContext, raw json.RawMessage) (any, error) {
        a, err := decode[struct {
                TeamID   string `json:"team_id"`
                DateFrom string `json:"date_from"`
                DateTo   string `json:"date_to"`
        }](raw)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        actor, ok := m.dir.ByID(cc.OnBehalfOf)
        if !ok || (actor.TeamID != a.TeamID && actor.Role != directory.RoleHRBP) {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("forbidden", "календарь доступен только участникам команды")
        }
        team, ok := m.cat.Teams[a.TeamID]
        if !ok {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("not_found", "команда не найдена")
        }
        out := corp.TeamEvents{Events: []catalog.CalendarEvent{}, AbsentByDate: map[string]int{}}
        for _, e := range team.Calendar {
                if e.Date >= a.DateFrom && e.Date <= a.DateTo {
                        out.Events = append(out.Events, e)
                }
        }
        m.mu.Lock()
        defer m.mu.Unlock()
        for _, r := range m.st.Absences {
                e, ok := m.dir.ByID(r.EmployeeID)
                if !ok || e.TeamID != a.TeamID || r.Status == "rejected" || r.EmployeeID == actor.ID {
                        continue
                }
                from, _ := parseDate(r.DateFrom)
                to, _ := parseDate(r.DateTo)
                for d := from; !d.After(to); d = d.AddDate(0, 0, 1) {
                        ds := d.Format("2006-01-02")
                        if ds >= a.DateFrom && ds <= a.DateTo {
                                out.AbsentByDate[ds]++
                        }
                }
        }
        return out, nil
}

func (m *Mock) accessCatalog(context.Context, mcp.CallContext, json.RawMessage) (any, error) {
        return map[string]any{"systems": m.cat.Access}, nil
}

func (m *Mock) accessCreate(_ context.Context, cc mcp.CallContext, raw json.RawMessage) (any, error) {
        a, err := decode[corp.AccessCreate](raw)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if cc.OnBehalfOf != a.EmployeeID {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("forbidden", "доступ можно запросить только для себя")
        }
        emp, err := m.person(a.EmployeeID)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        sys, ok := m.cat.AccessSystem(a.System)
        if !ok {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("unknown_system", "система %s отсутствует в каталоге", a.System)
        }
        if slices.Contains(sys.ForbiddenFor, emp.Role) {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("forbidden", "%s недоступен для вашей роли", sys.Title)
        }
        m.mu.Lock()
        if id, ok := m.st.Idem[a.IdempotencyKey]; ok && a.IdempotencyKey != "" {
                r := *m.st.Access[id]
                m.mu.Unlock()
                return r, nil
        }
        r := &corp.AccessRequest{RequestID: m.nextID("ACC"), EmployeeID: emp.ID, System: sys.ID, Level: sys.Level, CreatedAt: m.now().Format(time.RFC3339)}
        if sys.Approval == "auto" {
                r.Status = "granted"
        } else {
                r.Status = "pending_approval"
                if lead, ok := m.dir.LeadOf(emp.TeamID); ok {
                        r.ApproverID, r.ApproverName = lead.ID, lead.Name
                }
        }
        m.st.Access[r.RequestID] = r
        m.st.Idem[a.IdempotencyKey] = r.RequestID
        out := *r
        m.mu.Unlock()
        m.save()
        return out, nil
}

func (m *Mock) accessGet(_ context.Context, cc mcp.CallContext, raw json.RawMessage) (any, error) {
        a, err := decode[struct {
                RequestID string `json:"request_id"`
        }](raw)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        m.mu.Lock()
        defer m.mu.Unlock()
        r, ok := m.st.Access[a.RequestID]
        if !ok {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("not_found", "заявка %s не найдена", a.RequestID)
        }
        if !m.canRead(cc.OnBehalfOf, r.EmployeeID, r.ApproverID) {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("forbidden", "нет прав на просмотр заявки")
        }
        return *r, nil
}

func (m *Mock) accessDecide(_ context.Context, cc mcp.CallContext, raw json.RawMessage) (any, error) {
        a, err := decode[decideArgs](raw)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        m.mu.Lock()
        r, ok := m.st.Access[a.RequestID]
        if !ok {
                m.mu.Unlock()
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("not_found", "заявка %s не найдена", a.RequestID)
        }
        if err := m.checkLead(cc.OnBehalfOf, r.EmployeeID); err != nil {
                m.mu.Unlock()
                return nil, err
        }
        if r.Status == "pending_approval" {
                switch a.Decision {
                case "approve":
                        r.Status = "granted"
                case "reject":
                        r.Status = "rejected"
                default:
                        m.mu.Unlock()
                        return nil, mcp.Errorf("invalid_arguments", "decision должен быть approve или reject")
                }
        }
        out := *r
        m.mu.Unlock()
        m.save()
        return out, nil
}

func (m *Mock) checklistComplete(_ context.Context, cc mcp.CallContext, raw json.RawMessage) (any, error) {
        a, err := decode[corp.ChecklistComplete](raw)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        emp, err := m.person(a.EmployeeID)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if cc.OnBehalfOf != emp.ID && cc.OnBehalfOf != emp.MentorID && cc.OnBehalfOf != emp.LeadID {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("forbidden", "отметить пункт может стажёр, его наставник или руководитель")
        }
        if a.ItemID == "" {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("invalid_arguments", "item_id обязателен")
        }
        m.mu.Lock()
        items := m.st.Checklist[emp.ID]
        if items == nil {
                items = map[string]*corp.ChecklistItem{}
                m.st.Checklist[emp.ID] = items
        }
        it, ok := items[a.ItemID]
        if !ok || it.Status != "done" {
                it = &corp.ChecklistItem{EmployeeID: emp.ID, ItemID: a.ItemID, Status: "done", Evidence: a.Evidence, CompletedBy: cc.OnBehalfOf, CompletedAt: m.now().Format(time.RFC3339)}
                items[a.ItemID] = it
        }
        out := *it
        m.mu.Unlock()
        m.save()
        return out, nil
}

func (m *Mock) checklistGet(_ context.Context, cc mcp.CallContext, raw json.RawMessage) (any, error) {
        a, err := decode[struct {
                EmployeeID string `json:"employee_id"`
        }](raw)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        emp, err := m.person(a.EmployeeID)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if cc.OnBehalfOf != emp.ID && cc.OnBehalfOf != emp.MentorID && cc.OnBehalfOf != emp.LeadID {
                return nil, mcp.Errorf("forbidden", "нет прав на чек-лист")
        }
        m.mu.Lock()
        defer m.mu.Unlock()
        out := []corp.ChecklistItem{}
        for _, it := range m.st.Checklist[emp.ID] {
                out = append(out, *it)
        }
        slices.SortFunc(out, func(a, b corp.ChecklistItem) int {
                if a.ItemID < b.ItemID {
                        return -1
                }
                return 1
        })
        return map[string]any{"items": out}, nil
}

// Handler returns the HTTP handler: /mcp, /healthz and token-protected /admin/*.
func (m *Mock) Handler(token string) http.Handler {
        mux := http.NewServeMux()
        mux.Handle("/mcp", m.Server)
        mux.HandleFunc("GET /healthz", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) { _, _ = w.Write([]byte("ok")) })
        admin := func(fn func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request)) http.HandlerFunc {
                return func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
                        if token != "" && r.Header.Get("Authorization") != "Bearer "+token {
                                http.Error(w, "unauthorized", http.StatusUnauthorized)
                                return
                        }
                        fn(w, r)
                }
        }
        mux.HandleFunc("POST /admin/reset", admin(func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) { m.Reset(); w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent) }))
        mux.HandleFunc("POST /admin/fault", admin(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
                var b struct {
                        Enabled bool `json:"enabled"`
                }
                if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&b); err != nil {
                        http.Error(w, "bad body", http.StatusBadRequest)
                        return
                }
                m.SetFault(b.Enabled)
                w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
        }))
        return mux
}

package corpmock

import (
        "bytes"
        "io"
)

func bytesReader(b []byte) io.Reader { return bytes.NewReader(b) }

// Package cron imitates Avito's internal AI-cron: proactive jobs that make the
// buddy speak first — a morning brief, reminders about overdue steps, automatic
// nudges to mentors when an intern waits on them, a mentor question digest and
// polling of corporate services for decisions. Jobs are idempotent (cron_runs)
// and run on a single leader across replicas (Redis lock).
package cron

import (
        "context"
        "fmt"
        "log/slog"
        "os"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "github.com/google/uuid"

        "tropa/internal/actions"
        "tropa/internal/clock"
        "tropa/internal/directory"
        "tropa/internal/domain"
        "tropa/internal/notify"
        "tropa/internal/ratelimit"
        "tropa/internal/route"
        "tropa/internal/store"
)

// Cron runs proactive jobs.
type Cron struct {
        st      *store.Store
        clk     clock.Clock
        dir     *directory.Directory
        rt      *route.Engine
        actions *actions.Service
        notif   *notify.Notifier
        lock    ratelimit.Locker
        log     *slog.Logger
        owner   string
}

// New creates the scheduler.
func New(st *store.Store, clk clock.Clock, dir *directory.Directory, rt *route.Engine, as *actions.Service, n *notify.Notifier, lock ratelimit.Locker, log *slog.Logger) *Cron {
        host, _ := os.Hostname()
        return &Cron{st: st, clk: clk, dir: dir, rt: rt, actions: as, notif: n, lock: lock, log: log, owner: host + "-" + uuid.NewString()[:8]}
}

// Run ticks until ctx is done.
func (c *Cron) Run(ctx context.Context, every time.Duration) {
        t := time.NewTicker(every)
        defer t.Stop()
        for {
                if ok, err := c.lock.TryLock(ctx, "cron-leader", c.owner, every*3); err == nil && ok {
                        if err := c.Tick(ctx); err != nil {
                                c.log.Warn("cron tick", "err", err)
                        }
                }
                select {
                case <-ctx.Done():
                        return
                case <-t.C:
                }
        }
}

// Tick runs all jobs once.
func (c *Cron) Tick(ctx context.Context) error {
        if _, err := c.actions.Sync(ctx); err != nil {
                c.log.Warn("sync external", "err", err)
        }
        now := c.clk.Now()
        if now.Hour() < 9 {
                return nil
        }
        day := now.Format("2006-01-02")
        for _, in := range c.dir.Interns() {
                v, err := c.rt.Build(ctx, in, in, now)
                if err != nil {
                        return err
                }
                if err := c.brief(ctx, in, v, day); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
                if err := c.overdue(ctx, in, v); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        return c.digest(ctx, day)
}

func (c *Cron) brief(ctx context.Context, in *directory.Person, v route.View, day string) error {
        ok, err := c.st.TryMarkRun(ctx, "brief", in.ID+"|"+day, c.clk.Now())
        if err != nil || !ok {
                return err
        }
        var b strings.Builder
        fmt.Fprintf(&b, "Доброе утро, %s! День %d стажировки (%s). Пройдено %d из %d.", in.FirstName(), v.Day, v.Today, v.Done, v.Total)
        if v.Overdue > 0 {
                fmt.Fprintf(&b, " Просрочено: %d — начни с них.", v.Overdue)
        }
        if len(v.Waiting) > 0 {
                b.WriteString(" Ждём: " + v.Waiting[0].Title + ".")
        }
        payload := domain.MessagePayload{Intent: "morning_brief", Mode: "offline", Suggestions: []string{"Что мне делать дальше?"}}
        if len(v.Next) > 0 {
                payload.Cards = append(payload.Cards, domain.Card{Type: "steps", Title: "План на сегодня", Steps: v.Next})
        }
        if len(v.Waiting) > 0 && len(v.WhileWaiting) > 0 {
                payload.Cards = append(payload.Cards, domain.Card{Type: "steps", Title: "Пока ждёшь — можно сделать", Steps: v.WhileWaiting})
        }
        if err := c.st.InsertMessage(ctx, domain.Message{ID: uuid.NewString(), UserID: in.ID, Role: "buddy", Text: b.String(), Payload: payload, CreatedAt: c.clk.Now()}); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        next := "Все пункты на сегодня закрыты."
        if len(v.Next) > 0 {
                next = "Главное сегодня: " + v.Next[0].Title
        }
        c.notif.Notify(ctx, in.ID, "brief", fmt.Sprintf("День %d: утренний бриф", v.Day), next, "chat")
        c.notif.Track(ctx, in, "brief_sent", map[string]any{"day": v.Day})
        return nil
}

func (c *Cron) overdue(ctx context.Context, in *directory.Person, v route.View) error {
        for _, st := range v.Stages {
                for _, t := range st.Tasks {
                        if !t.Overdue || t.Status == domain.TaskDone {
                                continue
                        }
                        ok, err := c.st.TryMarkRun(ctx, "overdue", in.ID+"|"+t.ID, c.clk.Now())
                        if err != nil {
                                return err
                        }
                        if !ok {
                                continue
                        }
                        switch t.Owner {
                        case "intern":
                                if t.Status != domain.TaskBlocked && t.Status != domain.TaskWaiting {
                                        c.notif.Notify(ctx, in.ID, "reminder", "Просрочен пункт: «"+t.Title+"»", "Срок был "+t.DueDate+". Нужна помощь — спроси Тропу.", "task:"+t.ID)
                                }
                        default:
                                // "без неловкости": the buddy nudges the mentor/lead instead of the intern
                                ownerID := in.MentorID
                                if t.Owner == "lead" {
                                        ownerID = in.LeadID
                                }
                                c.notif.Notify(ctx, ownerID, "nudge", in.Name+" ждёт: «"+t.Title+"»", "Срок был "+t.DueDate+". Отметьте пункт в маршруте стажёра, когда сделаете.", "intern:"+in.ID)
                                c.notif.Track(ctx, in, "nudge_sent", map[string]any{"task": t.ID, "auto": true})
                        }
                }
        }
        return nil
}

func (c *Cron) digest(ctx context.Context, day string) error {
        for _, p := range c.dir.All() {
                if p.Role != directory.RoleMentor {
                        continue
                }
                qs, err := c.st.QuestionsForMentor(ctx, p.ID)
                if err != nil {
                        return err
                }
                open := 0
                for _, q := range qs {
                        if q.Status == domain.ExtOpen {
                                open++
                        }
                }
                if open == 0 {
                        continue
                }
                ok, err := c.st.TryMarkRun(ctx, "digest", p.ID+"|"+day, c.clk.Now())
                if err != nil {
                        return err
                }
                if ok {
                        c.notif.Notify(ctx, p.ID, "digest", fmt.Sprintf("Сводка: %d вопрос(ов) от стажёров", open), "Похожие вопросы уже объединены. Ответ можно сразу сохранить в базу знаний.", "questions")
                }
        }
        return nil
}

// Package directory imitates Avito's existing employee directory and role
// model. The case explicitly says real HR systems are out of scope: auth and
// roles already exist, so the prototype ships a synthetic, read-only directory.
package directory

import (
        "sort"
        "time"

        "tropa/internal/clock"
)

// Roles.
const (
        RoleIntern = "intern"
        RoleMentor = "mentor"
        RoleLead   = "lead"
        RoleHRBP   = "hrbp"
)

// Person is a synthetic employee.
type Person struct {
        ID           string    `json:"id"`
        Login        string    `json:"login"`
        Name         string    `json:"name"`
        Role         string    `json:"role"`
        Title        string    `json:"title"`
        TeamID       string    `json:"team_id"`
        MentorID     string    `json:"mentor_id,omitempty"`
        LeadID       string    `json:"lead_id,omitempty"`
        StartDate    time.Time `json:"start_date"`
